# #1073 June 1–10 OmniRoute row dispositions

Reviewed 2026-11-20 against public `cheaper-inference/OmniRoute` merged PR metadata and this checkout. Scope is the inclusive UTC window **2026-06-01 through 2026-06-10**. This records evidence and unresolved dispositions; it is not an owner selection list.

## Inventory verification

Re-ran the merged PR search in one bounded date slice: `repo:cheaper-inference/OmniRoute is:pr is:merged merged:2026-06-01..2026-06-10`. It returned **325 unique PR numbers**. The observed merge timestamps range from `2026-06-01T01:05:18Z` through `2026-06-10T21:16:57Z`; all 325 timestamps fall inside the requested UTC boundaries. Per-day counts: June 1 24, June 2 7, June 3 33, June 4 33, June 5 34, June 6 50, June 7 36, June 8 44, June 9 25, June 10 39 (sum 325). The earlier 325 claim is independently corroborated, not assumed.

Search was kept below GitHub's 1,000-result ceiling by using this ten-day interval. PR metadata was fetched through bounded batches of four. Initial PR-view file lists cap at 100; the 15 records at that cap were fetched again using the public paginated pull-request-files endpoint (two concurrent requests). The final inventory below has a complete file list for every row; no row has missing path pagination. Merge SHA is the exact `mergeCommit.oid`; head SHA is included when available. Titles are labels for navigation only and do not establish runtime behavior.

The existing Q1, May, and June scope reports are reconciled here: Q1 and May document aggregate windows plus selected bounded comparisons, while the prior June note explicitly lacked row-level metadata and final diffs. Their selected findings are not treated as June evidence unless this report's date-pinned row and final diff establish it. Existing port decisions and historical coverage records likewise do not prove a source PR mapping without a mapped source identity and local/landed evidence.

## Disposition rules and limits

“Out of scope” is used only where the complete source file list is documentation, upstream-specific deployment material, or CI/workflow files alone. “Provider Sign-in” is reserved for confirmed new-provider identity or sign-in changes; an auth-related filename alone is insufficient, and mixed or ambiguous records remain unresolved. A source title, label, architecture mismatch, or similar local filename is not proof of behavior. Dashboard files remain unresolved unless an existing surface and its behavior are verified; a new page is not treated as an existing-dashboard fix. No “already present” judgment is made from names alone.

Five existing-surface protocol/provider candidates were narrowly compared below from their final source diff to current local implementation. These comparisons identify only supported parity or a precise static gap; no runtime behavior was inferred. Focused units named in each note are evidence references, not tests run during this audit. No product files were changed.

## Narrow source-to-local comparisons

| Source PR | Final source diff and local comparison | Disposition / focused unit gap |
| --- | --- | --- |
| [#3107](https://github.com/cheaper-inference/OmniRoute/pull/3107), merge `98c59307c636e25cd07f8f0ec852bb6e1b79ff7a`, head `261a9108202ddc5763268bc8d6103bb0acdb8f4c` | Final diff adds native Codex passthrough preservation for `tool_search`, custom tools and `strict`, and avoids stripping internal assistant commentary only for native passthrough. Local surfaces are `open-sse/executors/codex.js`, `open-sse/translator/request/openai-responses.js`, and `tests/unit/codex-stateless-response-input.test.js`; their contracts include Responses history/tool normalization, but the source-specific native Codex `tool_search` and `custom` preservation branches were not verified as equivalent. | **Unresolved.** Focused unit gap: assert native Codex passthrough retains `tool_search`, valid named `custom` tools and `strict`, while translated/non-native calls still remove unsupported custom shapes; assert commentary is preserved only on native passthrough. |
| [#3108](https://github.com/cheaper-inference/OmniRoute/pull/3108), merge `0bd5c692317415c0c9521ed9d8e003287e65449c`, head `b57afb5bbe35a0f466cf4d5d9c27e23f24f8520c` | Final diff converts non-SSE `application/json` chat-completion responses into SSE for streaming requests and SSE-wraps semantic-cache hits. Local `open-sse/handlers/chatCore.js` has separate stream readiness and cache paths; no local `jsonToSse` equivalent appears in this tree. The source's exact response conversion is therefore not established as present. | **Unresolved protocol gap candidate.** Focused unit gap: pure offline checks for JSON upstream conversion preserving content, reasoning and tool calls; non-convertible JSON must retain existing error handling; streaming semantic cache hits must carry SSE content type and terminal frames. |
| [#3150](https://github.com/cheaper-inference/OmniRoute/pull/3150), merge `e4a9ec55821bc3baf3ae79499f1dd881cd22eaff`, head `32bb4b96523d7f880ecd0e547aa3485d654c624b` | Final diff normalizes legacy `image_url` parts in Responses `message.content` and replayed `output` shapes, mapping user images to `input_image` and assistant images to text. Local `open-sse/translator/request/openai-responses.js` maps standard Chat `image_url` content to Responses `input_image`; replayed `output` subparts are a distinct shape and were not found as the same sanitizer branch. | **Unresolved.** Focused unit gap: native Responses input sanitizer cases for user/assistant message content and replayed output arrays, preserving valid image detail and function-call output pairing. |
| [#3163](https://github.com/cheaper-inference/OmniRoute/pull/3163), merge `077d3672943e84a74d381f47d5cba34a6100446b`, head `cb94bf695d59eeee4adbf46b5dd7cf21d7ea8554` | Final diff adds MiniMax M3/M2.7 output metadata and case-insensitive lookup to upstream `modelSpecs.ts`. Local model contract is split across `open-sse/providers/vendorModelCapabilities.js`, `open-sse/providers/thinkingLevels.js`, and provider registries. Current local static metadata includes MiniMax M3/M2.7 context/reasoning facts but has `maxOutput: null` for both; the upstream numerical ceilings 512,000/256,000 are not locally evidenced here. | **Unresolved; do not transplant unverified ceilings.** Focused unit gap: test alias/case resolution and output cap only after an authoritative local/provider contract establishes the ceiling; current source diff cites external model claims but this audit does not independently validate them. |
| [#3122](https://github.com/cheaper-inference/OmniRoute/pull/3122), merge `84b5caeeb946d12223bee891c82ce640b46e6542`, head `0f433e06c61e6edc31b8b6d1121861a7b8e6ba20` | Final diff bounds Antigravity short 429 retries, parses “Individual quota reached … Resets in …” and caps cooldown at 30 days. Local `open-sse/executors/antigravity.js` and `open-sse/services/accountFallback.js` have retry/quota paths, but the exact bounded per-URL retry counter and this phrasing/cap were not verified in those implementations. | **Unresolved.** Focused unit gap: exercise static retry bound across URLs and exact reset-duration parsing/capping; distinguish quota exhaustion from transient rate limits and ensure the parsed deadline reaches the account/model lockout path. |

These five comparisons are not an exhaustive review of the 325 final diffs. None is marked already present. Rows outside this bounded comparison remain unresolved unless their complete path semantics support the narrow out-of-scope category below.
